		} "json:\"forecastday\""

type application.App struct {
	ctx                           context.Context
	cancel                        func()
	options                       application.Options
	applicationEventListeners     map[uint][]*application.EventListener
	applicationEventListenersLock sync.RWMutex
	applicationEventHooks         map[uint][]*application.eventHook
	applicationEventHooksLock     sync.RWMutex
	Window                        *application.WindowManager
	ContextMenu                   *application.ContextMenuManager
	KeyBinding                    *application.KeyBindingManager
	Browser                       *application.BrowserManager
	Env                           *application.EnvironmentManager
	Dialog                        *application.DialogManager
	Event                         *application.EventManager
	Menu                          *application.MenuManager
	Screen                        *application.ScreenManager
	Clipboard                     *application.ClipboardManager
	SystemTray                    *application.SystemTrayManager
	Autostart                     *application.AutostartManager
	GlobalShortcut                *application.GlobalShortcutManager
	Updater                       *updater.Updater
	windows                       map[uint]application.Window
	windowsLock                   sync.RWMutex
	systemTrays                   map[uint]*application.SystemTray
	systemTraysLock               sync.Mutex
	systemTrayID                  uint
	systemTrayIDLock              sync.RWMutex
	menuItems                     map[uint]*application.MenuItem
	menuItemsLock                 sync.Mutex
	starting                      bool
	running                       bool
	runLock                       sync.Mutex
	pendingRun                    []application.runnable
	bindings                      *application.Bindings
	impl                          application.platformApp
	applicationMenu               *application.Menu
	clipboard                     *application.Clipboard
	customEventProcessor          *application.EventProcessor
	Logger                        *slog.Logger
	contextMenus                  map[string]*application.ContextMenu
	contextMenusLock              sync.RWMutex
	assets                        *assetserver.AssetServer
	startURL                      string
	windowCreatedCallbacks        []func(application.Window)
	pid                           int
	capabilities                  capabilities.Capabilities
	isDebugMode                   bool
	keyBindings                   map[string]func(application.Window)
	keyBindingsLock               sync.RWMutex
	performingShutdown            bool
	shutdownLock                  sync.Mutex
	serviceShutdownLock           sync.Mutex
	shutdownTasks                 []func()
	application.platformSignalHandler
	wailsEventListenerLock sync.Mutex
	wailsEventListeners    []application.WailsEventListener
	singleInstanceManager  *application.singleInstanceManager
	messageProcessor       *application.MessageProcessor
}

	needsContext bool
	isVariadic   bool
}

	AttachModal(application.Window)
	Bounds() application.Rect

	Close()

	HideMenuBar()
	ID() uint
	Info(string, []interface{})
	InitiateFrontendDropProcessing([]string, int, int)
	IsFocused() bool
	IsFullscreen() bool
	IsIgnoreMouseEvents() bool
	IsMaximised() bool
	IsMinimised() bool
	IsVisible() bool
	Maximise() application.Window
	Minimise() application.Window
	Name() string
	NativeWindow() unsafe.Pointer
	OnWindowEvent(uint, func(*application.WindowEvent)) func()
	OpenContextMenu(*application.ContextMenuData)
	OpenDevTools()
	Position() (int, int)
	Print() error
	RegisterHook(uint, func(*application.WindowEvent)) func()
	RelativePosition() (int, int)
	Reload()
	Resizable() bool
	Restore()
	Run()
	SetAlwaysOnTop(bool) application.Window
	SetBackgroundColour(application.RGBA) application.Window
	SetBounds(application.Rect)
	SetCloseButtonState(int) application.Window
	SetContentProtection(bool) application.Window
	SetEnabled(bool)
	SetFrameless(bool) application.Window
	SetFullscreenButtonState(int) application.Window
	SetHTML(string) application.Window
	SetIgnoreMouseEvents(bool) application.Window
	SetMaxSize(int, int) application.Window
	SetMaximiseButtonState(int) application.Window
	SetMenu(*application.Menu)
	SetMinSize(int, int) application.Window
	SetMinimiseButtonState(int) application.Window
	SetPosition(int, int)
	SetRelativePosition(int, int) application.Window
	SetResizable(bool) application.Window
	SetScreen(*application.Screen) application.Window
	SetSize(int, int) application.Window
	SetTitle(string) application.Window
	SetURL(string) application.Window
	SetZoom(float64) application.Window
	Show() application.Window
	ShowMenuBar()
	Size() (int, int)
	SnapAssist()
	ToggleFrameless()
	ToggleFullscreen()
	ToggleMaximise()
	ToggleMenuBar()
	UnFullscreen()
	UnMaximise()
	UnMinimise()
	Width() int
	Zoom()

	ZoomOut()
	ZoomReset() application.Window
	copy()
	cut()
	delete()

	paste()
	redo()
	selectAll()
	shouldUnconditionallyClose() bool
	undo()

type updater.Host interface {
	Emit(string, []interface{}) bool
	OnEvent(string, func(interface{})) func()

	Quit()
}
type updater.Provider interface {
	Check(context.Context, updater.CheckRequest) (*updater.Release, error)
	Download(context.Context, *updater.Release, io.Writer, func(int64, int64)) error
	Name() string
}

	stagingDir     string

	dlMu           sync.Mutex
	sessMu         sync.Mutex
	session        *updater.windowSession
	periodicCtx    context.Context
	periodicCancel func()
	periodicDone   chan struct{}
}

	opacitySnapshots    map[uintptr]main.windowManagementOpacitySnapshot
	managedSnapshots    map[uintptr]main.WindowFullscreenSnapshot
	borderlessSnapshots []main.WindowFullscreenSnapshot
	fullscreenSnapshots []main.WindowFullscreenSnapshot
	lastError           string
}

type main.windowsLauncherGlobalHotkeyManager struct {
	callback             func(string)
	commands             chan main.launcherGlobalHotkeyCommand
	ready                chan struct{}
	threadID             uint32
	keyboardHook         uintptr
	keyboardHookCallback uintptr
	keyboardHookBindings []main.launcherGlobalHotkeyRegistration
	printScreenKeyDown   bool
	closeOnce            sync.Once
}

type main.windowsOLEDBlackoutCursorController struct {
	commands  chan main.oledBlackoutCursorCommand
	ready     chan struct{}
	done      chan struct{}
	call      func(bool) (int32, error)
	lock      sync.Mutex
	closed    bool
	closeErr  error
	closeOnce sync.Once
}

type main.windowsOLEDBlackoutHotkeyManager struct {
	callback  func(main.oledBlackoutHotkeyEvent)
	commands  chan main.oledBlackoutHotkeyCommand
	ready     chan struct{}
	done      chan struct{}
	threadID  uint32
	closeOnce sync.Once
}

type main.workspaceDataMaintenanceGate struct {
	mu          sync.Mutex
	maintenance bool
	active      int
	idle        chan struct{}
}

type main.workspacePathIdentity struct {
	canonical string
	volume    uint64
	file      uint64
	valid     bool
}

type main.workspacePathInspection struct {
	canonical        string
	exists           bool
	identity         main.workspacePathIdentity
	ancestorIdentity main.workspacePathIdentity
}

type struct { ref *string; value string } struct{
	ref *string
	value string
}

*libc.TLS

*libc.ThreadAdapter

*libc.file

*libc.pthreadData

type libc.TLS struct {
	errnop      uintptr
	allocaStack [][]uintptr
	allocas     []uintptr
	jumpBuffers []uintptr
	lastError   uint32
	libc.pthreadData
	sp                 int
	stack              libc.stackHeader
	ID                 int32
	reentryGuard       int32
	stackHeaderBalance int32
}

type libc.ThreadAdapter struct {
	token      uintptr
	tls        *libc.TLS
	param      uintptr
	threadFunc func(*libc.TLS, uintptr) uint32
}

type libc.file struct {
	_fd    int32
	hadErr bool
	t      uintptr
	uintptr
}

type libc.pthreadData struct {
	done     chan struct{}
	kv       map[uint32]uintptr
	retVal   uintptr
	wait     chan struct{}
	detached bool
}

type libc.stackHeader struct {
	free int
	page uintptr
	prev uintptr
	next uintptr
	sp   uintptr
	_    libc.stackHeaderPadding
}

type libc.stackHeaderPadding struct {
	a uintptr
}

*sqlite.Backup

*sqlite.Driver

*sqlite.Error

*sqlite.SQLitePreUpdateData

*sqlite.conn

*sqlite.result

*sqlite.rows

*sqlite.stmt

*sqlite.tx

type sqlite.AggregateFunction interface {
	Final(*sqlite.FunctionContext)
	Step(*sqlite.FunctionContext, []driver.Value) error
	WindowInverse(*sqlite.FunctionContext, []driver.Value) error
	WindowValue(*sqlite.FunctionContext) (driver.Value, error)
}
type sqlite.Backup struct {
	srcConn *sqlite.conn
	dstConn *sqlite.conn
	pBackup uintptr
}

type sqlite.Driver struct {
	udfs            map[string]*sqlite.userDefinedFunction
	collations      map[string]*sqlite.collation
	connectionHooks []func(sqlite.ExecQuerierContext, string) error
	modules         map[string]vtab.Module
}

type sqlite.Error struct {
	msg  string
	code int
}

type sqlite.ExecQuerierContext interface {
	ExecContext(context.Context, string, []driver.NamedValue) (driver.Result, error)
	QueryContext(context.Context, string, []driver.NamedValue) (driver.Rows, error)
}
type sqlite.FunctionContext struct {
	tls *libc.TLS
	ctx uintptr
}

type sqlite.SQLitePreUpdateData struct {
	tls          *libc.TLS
	pCsr         uintptr
	Op           int32
	DatabaseName string
	TableName    string
	OldRowID     int64
	NewRowID     int64
}

type sqlite.collation struct {
	zName uintptr
	pApp  uintptr
	enc   int32
}

type sqlite.conn struct {
	db  uintptr
	tls *libc.TLS
	sync.Mutex
	writeTimeFormat   string
	beginMode         string
	loc               *time.Location
	intToTime         bool
	textToTime        bool
	integerTimeFormat string
}

type sqlite.goCursor struct {
	table *sqlite.goTable
	impl  vtab.Cursor
}

type sqlite.goModule struct {
	name string
	impl vtab.Module
}

type sqlite.goTable struct {
	mod  *sqlite.goModule
	impl vtab.Table
}

type sqlite.result struct {
	lastInsertID int64
	rowsAffected int
}

type sqlite.rows struct {
	allocs    []uintptr
	c         *sqlite.conn
	columns   []string
	pstmt     uintptr
	doStep    bool
	empty     bool
	reuseStmt bool
}

type sqlite.stmt struct {
	c     *sqlite.conn
	psql  uintptr
	pstmt uintptr
}

type sqlite.tx struct {
	c *sqlite.conn
}

type sqlite.userDefinedFunction struct {
	zFuncName uintptr
	nArg      int32
	eTextRep  int32
	pApp      uintptr
	scalar    bool
	freeOnce  sync.Once
}

*vtab.Context

type vtab.Context struct {
	declare           func(string) error
	constraintSupport func() error
	config            func(int32, []int32) error
}

type vtab.Cursor interface {
	Close() error
	Column(int) (driver.Value, error)
	Eof() bool
	Filter(int, string, []driver.Value) error
	Next() error
	Rowid() (int64, error)
}
type vtab.Module interface {
	Connect(vtab.Context, []string) (vtab.Table, error)
	Create(vtab.Context, []string) (vtab.Table, error)
}
type vtab.Table interface {
	BestIndex(*vtab.IndexInfo) error
	Destroy() error
	Disconnect() error
	Open() (vtab.Cursor, error)
}
